### [160] [Intermediate scattering function of Brownian particles in a tilted cosine potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12421)
*Regina Rusch,Thomas Franosch*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文求解了倾斜余弦势中布朗粒子的Fokker-Planck方程，推导出广义中间散射函数(ISF)，通过Bloch定理和谱理论框架数值计算本征函数与本征值，并利用含时微扰理论展开ISF以获得低阶矩（均方位移、时变扩散系数、偏度、非高斯参数），通过布朗动力学模拟验证，并与谐振近似和确定性极限进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在倾斜周期性势场中研究布朗粒子的完整时空动力学，超越仅关注均方位移或扩散系数的传统方法，期望通过中间散射函数捕捉系统更丰富的空间相关性信息，填补该模型在空间-时间关联函数层面的理论研究空白。

Method: 基于一维过阻尼布朗粒子模型，求解倾斜余弦势中的Fokker-Planck方程；利用系统周期性通过Bloch定理将问题转化到谱理论框架；数值计算本征函数与本征值进而获得ISF和概率密度；采用含时微扰理论展开ISF推导低阶矩；通过布朗动力学模拟验证结果。

Result: 推导出包含两个波矢的广义ISF，获得了均方位移、时变扩散系数、偏度及非高斯参数等低阶矩的解析表达式；数值结果与布朗动力学模拟吻合；分析了不同倾斜力强度下的行为，并与谐振近似和确定性极限进行了系统对比。

Conclusion: 该谱理论框架为倾斜周期势中布朗运动的时空关联提供了完整而精确的描述，广义ISF和低阶矩为理解该系统在热涨落与外力竞争下的输运和非平衡行为提供了深入视角，验证了理论方法在不同倾斜力区间的有效性。

Abstract: We solve the Fokker-Planck equation for a Brownian particle in a tilted cosine potential and derive the intermediate scattering function (ISF), which captures the full spatio-temporal dynamics of the system. The model consists of a single overdamped Brownian particle in one dimension. We derive a generalized ISF comprising two wave vectors to describe correlations in the periodic potential. Exploiting the periodicity via Bloch's theorem, we formulate the problem within a spectral-theoretical framework and numerically compute the corresponding eigenfunctions and eigenvalues, from which we obtain the ISF and the probability density. Using time-dependent perturbation theory, we expand the ISF and derive low-order moments, including the mean-square displacement, time-dependent diffusivity, skewness, and the non-Gaussian parameter. Our analytical results are validated by Brownian-dynamics simulations and analyzed focussing on different regimes of the tilting force. The results are compared to a harmonic approximation and the deterministic limit.

</details>


### [161] [Thermodynamics of Learning: A Typed Four-Component Accounting of Memory, Fit, and Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12791)
*Akihito Sudo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为有限状态学习设备建立了一套类型化会计框架，将训练侧拟合函数、记录相关存量、更新侧搜索账本和运行资本价值四个组件分离。核心贡献包括：(I) 分离定理——记录相关与世界相关可增长n ln 2而资本增益恰为零；(II) 资本化账本——给出flat*提取恒等式和普适账本恒等式，在特定稳定条件下推导出资本化效率上界η_cap≤1及其等号充要条件；(III) 价值保持——给出两层对齐域，建立价值与侧信息调整后的记录拟合之间的精确汇率，以及在联合侧信息中性条件下的原始记录存量汇率。这些是关于任务分布漂移下有限设备价值保持的陈述，而非统计泛化理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文指出有限学习设备已记录的内容与对未来任务有价值的内容并非同一数量。传统学习理论关注记录与世界相关的增长，但真正重要的是这些记录能否转化为执行新任务时的资本价值。作者旨在建立一套严谨的会计框架，区分训练侧拟合、记录相关存量、更新侧搜索过程与运营资本价值，从而揭示设备学习效能中容易混淆的四个维度。

Method: 作者发展了一种类型化的有限状态学习设备会计方法，将学习设备分解为四个可分离组件：训练侧拟合函数Φ_fit、记录相关存量J_D=I(M;D)、更新侧搜索账本σ_M和运营资本价值V(M;T,b)。通过定义"工作间隙"（信息协议类与删除记忆读取端口后重新优化的盲目类之间的差异）来量化资本价值。利用flat*机制、F5'稳定M-局部更新、无丢弃记录相关条件(f)等假设，推导出资本化效率的精确上界和等号条件；通过两层对齐域分析价值保持，引入侧信息调整汇率和联合中性条件。

Result: (I) 分离定理：存在设备族使记录相关与世界相关增长n ln 2但资本增益为零；在flat*区域，无数据更新不会增加V。(II) 由flat*提取恒等式和普适账本恒等式，在(F5')稳定的M-局部更新和无丢弃记录相关条件下，资本化效率η_cap=ΔV/(kT σ_M)满足η_cap≤1，并给出等号的充要条件。(III) 保留差距L_gen和保留率ρ_gen的分析中，给出精确汇率I(M';D|Y)以及联合中性条件(M,D)⊥Y下的原始记录存量汇率，以一次性密码本为边界见证。

Conclusion: 这些结果为有限状态学习设备在任务分布漂移下的价值保持提供了精确的形式化刻画。关键洞见是"记录相关"与"资本价值"必须严格区分：设备可以积累大量记录相关但资本增益为零，因此评估学习设备不能仅看其记录了多少信息，而要看这些记录能否通过更新协议转化为可操作的运营价值。该框架是面向任务的有限设备分析，不声称构成统计泛化的一般理论。

Abstract: What a finite learning device has recorded and what will hold value for it on future tasks are not the same quantity. We develop a typed accounting for finite-state learning devices that separates four components: a training-side fit functional $Φ_{\mathrm{fit}}$, the record-correlation stock $J_{D}=I(M;D)$, an update-side search ledger $σ_{M}$, and an operational capital value $V(M;T,b)$. This value is the work gap between an informed protocol class and a blind class obtained by deleting the memory-read port and re-optimizing from scratch. (I) Separation: for every $n$, there is a device family on which record correlation and world correlation grow by $n\ln 2$ while the capital gain is exactly zero. In the $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ regime, data-free updates never increase $V$. (II) Capitalization ledger: an exact $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ extraction identity and a universal ledger identity give, for (F5$'$)-stable $M$-local updates under a no-discarded-record-correlation condition (f), the bound $η_{\mathrm{cap}}\le 1$ for the capitalization efficiency $η_{\mathrm{cap}}=ΔV/(k T\,σ_{M})$, together with necessary and sufficient conditions for equality. (III) Value retention: for the retention gap $L_{\mathrm{gen}}$ and retention ratio $ρ_{\mathrm{gen}}$ (the former carries no sign constraint; the latter is defined for positive training-side value and is not confined to $[0,1]$) we give a two-layer alignment domain: an exact exchange rate between value and the side-information-adjusted record fit $I(M';D\mid Y)$ without any record-side-information independence assumption, and a raw record-stock exchange rate under a joint side-information neutrality condition $(M,D)\perp Y$, whose boundary is marked by an explicit one-time-pad witness. These are statements about finite-device value retention under task-distribution shift, not a theory of statistical generalization.

</details>


### [162] [Local and quasilocal conservation laws of three-state IRF cellular automata and their quantum deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13080)
*Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用补丁矩阵乘积方法研究了Klobas和Prosen提出的两种可逆三态面相互作用型元胞自动机（物种保持规则与物种翻转规则）及其量子形变。研究发现两类经典规则共享一个具有辅助维度三实现的一参数拟局域守恒律族，其Euler速度为±√(3/23)，为该体系最大Euler速度提供了严格下界。物种翻转规则的结果与已有外推值0.361吻合，证实了已知声活性荷的完备性；物种保持规则还额外拥有生成族产生无限严格局域荷的塔结构。量子形变中，相同代数结构导致精确低键维不变态（疤痕候选）、指数级长寿命拟局域准模态及非零Mazur界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可积/近可积晶格系统的守恒律结构与输运行为是统计物理的核心问题。Klobas和Prosen提出的两类三态面相互作用型元胞自动机（species-preserving和species-flipping）代表了非平庸的相互作用可逆动力学，但其守恒律结构、Euler速度及量子形变性质尚未被完全刻画。本研究旨在通过补丁矩阵乘积方法系统地揭示这些规则中隐藏的代数结构和守恒量族，并建立经典与量子形变之间的统一框架。

Method: 采用补丁矩阵乘积（patch-matrix-product）方法，构建平移不变ansatz，分析三态面相互作用型元胞自动机的守恒律。通过辅助维度三的实现构造共享的一参数拟局域守恒律族；利用解析辅助维度二族的一阶中心对数导数识别正则成员；对守恒荷的闭包空间计算Euler速度；在量子形变中利用相同代数结构构造低键维不变态、准模态和Mazur界。

Result: (1)两类经典规则共享一参数拟局域守恒律族（辅助维度为3），每个正则成员是穿过恒等可观测量的一族解析辅助维度二守恒量的一阶中心对数导数；(2)在由该族及三个初等局域荷张成的闭包中，非零Euler速度仅为±√(3/23)，从而√(3/23)是更大守恒扇区中最大Euler速度的严格下界；(3)物种翻转规则下该值与Klobas和Prosen报告的外推值0.361一致，强烈支持声活性荷的完备性；(4)物种保持规则额外具有交错生成族，其对数导数形成无限严格局域荷塔；(5)量子形变中，相同代数结构产生精确低键维不变态（疤痕候选）、指数长寿命拟局域准模态和投影后产生非零Mazur界的对角拟局域荷。

Conclusion: 本研究通过补丁矩阵乘积方法将两类看似不同的三态面相互作用型元胞自动机统一在同一守恒律框架下，揭示了其Euler速度的精确界定（√(3/23)）及其与数值外推的一致性。量子形变中守恒结构的保持表明经典可积结构的代数根源在量子层面依然有效，为构造量子疤痕态和慢热化体系提供了新途径。该工作为高维辅助空间的守恒律构造及可积系统完备性判定提供了方法论范例。

Abstract: Using patch-matrix-product methods, we study two reversible three-state interaction-round-a-face cellular automata introduced by Klobas and Prosen [J. Phys. A 55, 094003 (2022)] - the species-preserving and species-flipping rules - and a coherent quantum deformation interpolating between them. Within an explicit translationally invariant ansatz, the two classical rules share a one-parameter family of quasi-local conservation laws with an auxiliary-dimension-three realization. Every regular member is also the first centered logarithmic derivative of an analytic auxiliary-dimension-two family passing through the identity observable. On the closed span of this family and the three elementary local charges, the only nonzero Euler velocities are $\pm\sqrt{3/23}$. Consequently, $\sqrt{3/23}$ is a rigorous lower bound on the maximal Euler speed in any larger conserved sector. For the species-flipping rule, this value agrees with the reported extrapolated value $0.361$ of Klobas and Prosen, strongly supporting completeness of the known sound-active charges. The species-preserving rule admits, in addition, a staggered generating family whose logarithmic derivatives form an infinite tower of strictly local charges. In the quantum deformation, the same algebraic structures yield exact low-bond-dimension invariant states (scar candidates), exponentially long-lived quasi-local quasimodes, and diagonal quasi-local charges that produce a nonzero Mazur bound after projection away from the three elementary local charges.

</details>


### [163] [Equivariant learning of a transferable three-dimensional classical density functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13506)
*Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文展示了一种直接从三维平衡密度场学习经典密度泛函理论（cDFT）中过量自由能泛函的方法，无需自由能或化学势标签。该方法保持空间对称性和变分一致性，学得的单个泛函可跨温度、系统尺寸和统计系综迁移，并能预测结构因子、状态方程、液-汽共存和界面展宽等未作为训练目标的性质，同时成功应用于复杂三维几何（如溶剂耗尽桥和螺旋形孔道吸附）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体的集体行为对热力学条件、界面和受限环境高度敏感，但预测每一种新状态通常需要单独进行原子模拟。经典密度泛函理论提供了可复用的变分描述，然而其核心的过量自由能泛函通常是未知的，且已有的学习型近似大多局限于平面或低维情境。

Method: 直接从全三维平衡密度场学习过量自由能泛函，同时保持空间对称性与变分一致性，并且不需要自由能或化学势标签作为监督信号。

Result: 单个学得的泛函可跨温度、系统尺寸和统计系综迁移；能重建结构因子、状态方程、液-汽共存和界面展宽等未参与训练的目标性质；在复杂三维几何中预测了胶体间溶剂耗尽桥形成与破裂时的非单调力，以及互连螺旋形孔道中的吸附行为。

Conclusion: 平衡密度数据可被转化为可迁移的热力学生成器，将微观液体结构连接至响应、相行为和集体现象。

Abstract: Liquids exhibit collective behavior that depends sensitively on thermodynamic conditions, interfaces and confinement, yet predicting each new state commonly requires a separate atomistic simulation. Classical density functional theory offers a reusable variational description, but its central excess free-energy functional is generally unknown, and learned approximations have largely remained restricted to planar or lower-dimensional settings. Here we show that this functional can be learned directly from fully three-dimensional equilibrium density fields while preserving spatial symmetry and variational consistency, without free-energy or chemical-potential labels. A single learned functional transfers across temperatures, system sizes and statistical ensembles, and recovers structure factors, the equation of state, liquid--vapor coexistence and interfacial broadening, none of which are used as training targets. Applied to complex three-dimensional geometries, it predicts the non-monotonic force associated with formation and rupture of a solvent-depleted bridge between colloids and adsorption in an interconnected gyroid pore. These results demonstrate that equilibrium density data can be converted into a transferable thermodynamic generator connecting microscopic liquid structure to response, phase behavior and collective phenomena.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将预训练黑盒预测函数的知识迁移到新异质输入空间的方法。方法将目标回归函数分解为可迁移成分（由黑盒提供信息）和不可迁移成分（捕捉新空间独有信息），通过两步神经网络过程进行估计。论文推导了预测风险界，证明在不可迁移成分较小或平滑时优于非迁移方法，并在额外条件下达到比仅用标注数据更低的多项式阶最坏情况风险，同时扩展到多黑盒聚合场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑盒预测函数包含了从大规模数据集和大量计算中提炼的知识，但当可用输入特征与黑盒期望的特征不一致时，无法直接利用这些知识。这限制了黑盒预测能力在不同特征空间之间的迁移，因此需要一种方法将黑盒中的预测知识转移到新的、异质的输入空间。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为两部分：(1)可迁移成分，由黑盒预测函数提供信息；(2)不可迁移成分，捕捉新输入空间独有的信息。采用两步神经网络流程：第一步利用大量无标注的特征对（桥接两个输入空间）估计可迁移成分；第二步利用有限的标注数据估计不可迁移成分。框架还可扩展到多个黑盒（各有自己的输入空间）场景，通过聚合降低预测误差。

Result: 推导出预测风险界，证明当不可迁移成分较小或光滑时，估计器优于非迁移方法，且能自适应于这两种情况。在额外条件下，估计器的最坏情况风险的多项式阶严格小于仅从标注数据估计的极小极大风险。对于多黑盒扩展，聚合可以减少预测误差。

Conclusion: 方法在模拟和真实数据上验证了实用性，展示了从预训练黑盒向异质输入空间进行知识迁移的有效性，并提供理论保证支持其优越性。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [165] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出SSPO方法，通过结构感知的相似度加权leave-one-out基线来联合评分所有采样解，解决偏好优化方法中的梯度信号极化和均值基线冗余两大问题，在TSP、EFL、JSP基准上取得一致提升，并已在京东生产系统中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化（NCO）方法依赖并行解采样进行训练，但未能充分利用协同采样解组中的丰富信息。偏好优化方法只锚定单个最优解，丢弃其他解的细粒度质量和结构信号（梯度信号极化问题）；均值基线则均匀加权所有解，导致结构近似的解冗余淹没基线并保持高梯度方差（基线冗余问题）。

Method: 提出SSPO（结构感知相似度加权偏好优化），通过不相似度加权的leave-one-out基线联合评分所有B个采样解：结构上不同的解获得更高权重，用一个统一机制同时解决极化与冗余两个失败。基线使用零参数的、问题自适应的解嵌入，由编码器已有的节点表示构建。

Result: 在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上的实验相比现有最佳锚定和均匀权重基线取得一致提升。与均匀RLOO在TSP和EFL上的直接对比证实结构感知加权是改进的主要驱动因素。SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东（JD.com）生产设施选址系统中部署，验证了规模化应用的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 结构感知的相似度加权机制能够有效解决NCO训练中梯度信号极化和基线冗余问题，既保留偏好优化的细粒度信号又避免均匀基线带来的冗余干扰，且在真实生产环境（京东设施选址）中得到验证。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [166] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布强化学习中分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题。在生成模型假设下，建立了同步和异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，证明其平均迭代弱收敛于重标度布朗运动，并提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，无需存储完整轨迹即可构建渐近枢轴统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中QTD算法的统计推断（如置信区间构建）尚缺乏理论保障，已有方法多需要存储完整迭代轨迹，内存开销大且难以在线实现高效推断，亟需建立QTD的渐近理论并提供可在线计算的推断方法。

Method: 首先在生成模型假设下，分别针对同步和异步QTD建立函数中心极限定理，证明平均迭代弱收敛于重标度的布朗运动；随后基于随机缩放（random scaling）原理构造渐近枢轴统计量，该统计量利用QTD整条路径信息进行推断，且可通过在线方式计算，无需存储完整迭代轨迹。

Result: 证明了同步和异步QTD迭代平均值的弱收敛性质，建立了功能中心极限定理；提出的基于随机缩放的推断统计量具有渐近枢轴性质，且可在线计算，显著降低了内存需求，实现了分布强化学习中高效的统计推断。

Conclusion: 该工作为QTD提供了系统的渐近推断理论框架，所提在线推断方法在保证统计有效性的同时大幅减少了内存开销，为分布强化学习的实际应用提供了实用的统计推断工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [167] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在目标环境（移位后的分布）下DRO和RS两种鲁棒学习方法的有限样本泛化误差界，提出了信息引导的超参数校准方法，并在网络批量订购问题中进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒学习框架（DRO和RS）的有限样本保证要么在源环境下建立，要么针对模糊集上的对抗最坏情况，缺乏对目标环境下泛化误差的系统分析及两种方法的比较。

Method: 1) 推导DRO和RS在移位目标环境下的有限样本泛化误差界，显式刻画鲁棒超参数带来的灵敏度降低与正则化惩罚之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中带来的维度灾难；2) 当部分移位信息（如移位幅度或方向）可用时，提出信息引导的超参数校准方法，在相同信息条件下比较两种方法；3) 将该框架应用于网络批量订购问题。

Result: 在部分信息机制下，DRO和RS表现出互补的理论与经验行为；该方法在需求分布正向移位时能解释鲁棒策略的响应方式。

Conclusion: 填补了分布移位下鲁棒学习方法统计性质的理解空白，为比较DRO与RS提供了原则性的基础。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [168] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维网络场景下（参数远多于观测值）的邻域选择问题，证明在线性模型假设下，最小描述长度（MDL）方法相比Lasso、AIC、BIC等传统方法能获得更保守的邻域估计（低假阳性率），并揭示了岭参数对均方误差的影响及其与机器学习中"双重下降"现象的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着网络分析中包含越来越多的变量以避免遗漏重要变量及其连接，高维场景下（参数远多于观测值）如何准确估计每个节点的邻域（即网络中的连接）成为一个关键挑战。传统邻域选择方法（如Lasso、AIC、BIC）容易产生大量虚假边（高假阳性率），需要寻找更可靠的估计方法。

Method: 论文通过线性模型进行邻域估计，分析两种情况：(i)真实模型为线性时的邻域选择；(ii)真实模型为非线性时需要对高维进行惩罚。研究了岭参数对均方误差的影响，并将其与机器学习中的"双重下降"现象相联系，最终提出在惩罚中纳入模型空间体积，利用最小描述长度（MDL）原理进行邻域选择。

Result: 证明在两种情况下（模型正确假设为线性或错误假设为线性），最小描述长度（MDL）方法都能在高维场景下实现正确的邻域选择或得到更小（低假阳性率）的邻域估计。相比之下，Lasso、AIC、BIC等常见方法会产生大量虚假边（高假阳性率），而MDL方法则能提供保守且可靠的估计。

Conclusion: 在高维网络场景下，为实现充分的邻域选择，需要将模型空间的体积纳入惩罚项。最小描述长度（MDL）方法能够有效克服传统选择方法的缺陷，在两个研究情形下都获得低假阳性率的邻域估计，为高维网络推断提供了更可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [169] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了特征参数化代价函数下逆最优传输（IOT）的统计与算法理论，核心贡献是Sinkhorn线性化及其谱代理公式，并据此提出了四个定理（可辨识性、稀疏一致性、适定性、收敛性）和一个误设定观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优传输问题旨在从观测到的传输计划中恢复代价函数参数，但缺乏系统的统计和算法理论支撑。本文希望为特征参数化代价C_theta(i,j)=-theta^T phi(i,j)下的IOT问题提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 核心技法是Sinkhorn线性化——即熵正则OT计划对代价的隐函数灵敏度——及其谱代理公式（形式上谱精确但几何上透明）。受限Hessian满足谱夹心不等式，导出核心界sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma))，驱动整个理论框架。

Result: T1: theta在规范核的商空间上全局单射，维度上界F<=(K-1)^2。T2: l1惩罚估计在不可表示条件和得分集中性下恢复真实支撑集，失败概率指数衰减。T3: 特征矩映射M(theta)强单调，逆映射Lipschitz常数L<=epsilon||Phi^T S_a||_op/(pi_min lambda_min(Sigma))。T4: 局部强凸性保证梯度下降单调收敛。O5: 误设定下估计器收敛到OT模型投影，Hölder连续性经验指数alpha_eff在(0,1)。

Conclusion: 该理论为特征参数化IOT提供了从可辨识性到算法收敛性的完整刻画，并揭示了在误设定情形下估计器仍具有投影收敛保证，为后续应用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [170] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein Filtering (WF)框架，通过从受污染数据集中筛选并丢弃可疑样本，恢复底层干净数据分布。WF选择使经验分布与完全污染经验分布之间的Wasserstein距离最大化的样本子集，从而隔离并移除几何上有影响的离群点。提出了三种算法（SinkMarg、SinkWF、SlicedWF），并在理论上证明了在FELP污染模型下WF估计器在具有有界协方差分布族上达到极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据常被污染（离群点或局部扰动），如何从受污染数据中恢复底层干净分布是一个核心挑战。现有方法往往需要特定模型假设或缺乏理论保证，因此需要一种模型无关、理论上最优且实用的样本筛选框架。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Filtering (WF)框架，核心思想是选取一个经验分布与完全污染经验分布之间Wasserstein距离最大化的样本子集，从而优先识别并剔除几何上有影响的离群点。为计算可处理性，提出三种算法：边际筛选方案SinkMarg，以及利用熵正则化最优传输和切片Wasserstein近似的联合优化算法SinkWF和SlicedWF。理论上引入Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP)污染模型，刻画远距离分离离群点和局部不可区分扰动的混合污染。

Result: 在FELP污染模型下证明WF估计器在具有有界协方差的分布族上达到极小极大最优性。广泛的数值实验表明WF在合成数据集、基准异常检测套件以及扩散模型的鲁棒生成学习上均表现出色，作为实用的模型无关预处理工具具有竞争性的离群检测性能和显著的生成建模下游收益。

Conclusion: WF是一个高度实用、模型无关的预处理工具，既能有效检测离群点，也能在重度污染下为生成建模带来实质性收益，并具备坚实的理论最优性保证。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers. To render this optimization computationally tractable, we introduce three algorithms: a marginal screening scheme, SinkMarg, and two joint optimization algorithms, SinkWF and SlicedWF, leveraging entropic optimal transport and sliced Wasserstein approximations, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce the Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) contamination model, which characterizes corruptions consisting of well-separated outliers and locally indistinguishable perturbations. Under this model, we prove that the WF estimator achieves minimax optimality over distribution families with bounded covariance. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets, benchmark anomaly detection suites, and robust generative learning with diffusion models demonstrate that WF serves as a highly practical, model-agnostic preprocessing tool. It delivers competitive outlier detection performance and provides substantial downstream benefits for generative modeling under heavy contamination.

</details>


### [171] [Bagging Robustly Learns VC Classes with Linear Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13514)
*Omar Montasser*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文重新审视对抗性鲁棒学习问题，证明了VC类可以在与VC维d成线性关系的样本复杂度下被对抗性鲁棒地学习，相较于Montasser等人（2019）的此前上界实现了指数级改进。该结果通过一个简单的非恰当算法实现——将Breiman（1996）的经典bagging（自举聚合）启发式方法与鲁棒经验风险最小化（RERM）相结合，在O(d*)个独立自举样本上计算RERM并取多数投票（d*为对偶VC维）。同时，作者给出了下界，证明在一般情况下的oracle模型中，任何学习器即使获得任意多的训练样本，也需要Ω(d*)次RERM oracle调用，表明该上界是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性鲁棒学习是机器学习中的关键挑战。尽管已有研究表明对抗性鲁棒学习可能比标准学习更困难（例如均匀分布上的紧致集分类问题），但Montasser等人（2019）提出的样本复杂度上界（可能涉及pseudo-dimension等复杂量）与VC维相比存在指数差距。本文的核心动机是弄清：能否将对抗性鲁棒学习的样本复杂度改进到与标准VC学习相当的线性水平？以及是否存在简单高效的算法实现这一目标？

Method: 作者提出了一种简单的非恰当（improper）学习算法，将经典bagging（自举聚合）与鲁棒经验风险最小化（RERM）结合：1）从训练数据中抽取O(d*)个独立bootstrap样本（d*为对偶VC维）；2）在每个bootstrap样本上运行RERM；3）对所有得到的假设进行多数投票作为最终输出。在证明上，作者利用对偶VC维与bagging的方差缩减特性，给出了该算法的对抗性鲁棒样本复杂度为O(d)的证明，并通过构造性下界论证了Ω(d*)次RERM oracle调用在此oracle模型中是不可避免的。

Result: 1) 证明了VC类在对抗性鲁棒学习中的样本复杂度为O(d)（线性于VC维），相比Montasser等人（2019）的结果实现指数级改进；2) 证明该线性样本复杂度通过简单的bagging+RERM多数投票算法即可达到，且该算法是非恰当的（可突破恰当学习的限制）；3) 证明了在RERM oracle模型下，Ω(d*)次oracle调用是任何算法所必需的，即使训练样本无限多，从而确立了该算法在oracle调用次数上的最优性。

Conclusion: 本文从根本上改进了对抗性鲁棒学习的样本复杂度上界，将其从指数级的pseudo-dimension相关量改进为线性的VC维，并且给出了达到该界的最优算法（bagging+RERM多数投票）。下界结果进一步表明，在oracle模型下该算法在RERM调用次数上也是最优的，说明对抗性鲁棒学习的意外简单性与标准学习相当。这一结果对理论和实践都有重要意义：理论层面澄清了对抗性鲁棒学习的样本复杂性本质，实践层面提供了一种简单、易于实现且高效的鲁棒学习算法。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of learning predictors robust to adversarial examples at test-time. We prove that VC classes are adversarially robustly learnable with sample complexity linear in the VC dimension $d$, providing an exponential improvement over the previous upper bound of Montasser, Hanneke, and Srebro (2019). Remarkably, this result is achieved with a simple improper algorithm that combines the classic heuristic bagging (bootstrap aggregation) of Breiman (1996) with robust empirical risk minimization (RERM). Our algorithm computes RERMs on $O(d^\star)$ independent bootstrap samples and outputs their majority vote, where $d^\star$ denotes the dual VC dimension. We complement this result with a lower bound showing that this is unavoidable: in general, any learner in this oracle model requires $Ω(d^\star)$ calls to an RERM oracle, even when given arbitrarily many training examples.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Monodromy of plane curve singularities and quiver mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12484)
*Roger Casals*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了可塑实Morsification的quiver突变类唯一决定平面曲线奇点的积分monodromy模，并建立了Fomin、Pylyavskyy、Shustin和Thurston猜想在可塑不可约情形下的代数到拓扑方向的蕴涵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在验证S. Fomin等人提出的猜想——该猜想假设Morsification的代数不变量（quiver突变类）足以决定平面曲线奇点的拓扑类型。这一猜想连接了代数表示论与奇异点理论两个领域。

Method: 作者发展了基于等变Euler配对的表示论技术，该配对定义在微分双分次Ginzburg代数的连续有限维dg模的导出范畴上。一个关键步骤是证明plabic fence的quiver突变类能唯一恢复相伴光滑链环的Alexander模的挠部分。

Result: 证明了可塑实Morsification的quiver突变类唯一决定平面曲线奇点的积分monodromy模；具体地，可塑divide的quiver突变类决定不可约平面曲线奇点的复拓扑类型。同时证明了plabic fence的quiver突变类唯一恢复光滑链环Alexander模的挠部分。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了Fomin-Pylyavskyy-Shustin-Thurston猜想在可塑不可约情形下代数到拓扑方向的蕴涵成立，为连接组合代数不变量与奇异点拓扑提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The main result of this article shows that the quiver mutation class of a malleable real Morsification uniquely determines the integral monodromy module of a plane curve singularity. In particular, we show that the quiver mutation class of a malleable divide determines the complex topological type of an irreducible plane curve singularity. This establishes the algebraic-to-topological implication of a conjecture of S.~Fomin, P.~Pylyavskyy, E.~Shustin and D.~Thurston in the malleable irreducible case. The result is proven by developing representation-theoretic techniques based on an equivariant Euler pairing in the derived category of continuous finite-dimensional dg modules over a differential bigraded Ginzburg algebra. A key step uses these techniques to show that the quiver mutation class of a plabic fence uniquely recovers the torsion part of the Alexander module of the associated smooth link.

</details>


### [173] [Virasoro Recursions for Simplicially Stable Curves with Colliding Markings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12507)
*You-Cheng Chou,Hsian-Hua Tseng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单纯稳定曲线模堆上的后代积分，将约化态射分解为基本墙穿越，从而用普通Witten-Kontsevich关联子重建公式，并推导出修正的Virasoro递推关系，最终将所有有限支撑理论打包到完备的不相交支撑Fock空间中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单纯稳定曲线模堆上的后代积分，旨在通过墙穿越分解建立与经典Witten-Kontsevich理论的联系，并进一步推导Virasoro递推结构。

Method: 将约化态射分解为初等墙穿越，利用重建公式将后代积分化为普通Witten-Kontsevich关联子；在完备的不相交支撑Fock空间中，将扩展势能表示为Witten-Kontsevich势能的累积量平移。

Result: 得到后代积分的重建公式，推导出修正的Virasoro递推，并在Fock空间中建立了扩展势能与Witten-Kontsevich势能之间的累积量平移关系。

Conclusion: 单纯稳定曲线模空间上的后代积分理论可以通过墙穿越分解与经典Witten-Kontsevich理论统一起来，并具有清晰的Virasoro与Fock空间结构。

Abstract: We study descendant integrals on moduli stacks of simplicially stable curves. For each finite collision complex, we factor the reduction morphism into elementary wall crossings and obtain a reconstruction formula in terms of ordinary Witten-Kontsevich correlators. We use the formula to derive corrected Virasoro recursions. We then package all finite-support theories in a completed disjoint-support Fock space, where the extended potential is a cumulant translate of the Witten-Kontsevich potential.

</details>


### [174] [Collision-Hull Compression for Homogeneous Keller Maps and a Forty-Variable Counterexample to Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12543)
*Thomas Prellberg*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向齐次Keller映射的、由碰撞生成的初等压缩原理。该构造将碰撞生成的子代数作为极化代数的载体，关键在于该子代数承载一个非单射的Keller限制，并控制标准对称提升的维数。对Thompson的24变量三次齐次映射，精确极化给出增长序列2,4,11,20,20，所得子代数恰为MacFarlane的20维不变子空间，从而从碰撞本身出发规范地恢复了已知的24-20压缩。文章还构造了一个显式的350单项式齐四次映射使Zhao消失猜想失效，并给出Q(i)上的精确梯度碰撞；在Q、Q(i)、C上不存在包含该碰撞的真不变线性子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobian猜想与Keller映射研究中，如何从碰撞（collision）出发系统地构造压缩或不变子空间是一个关键问题。现有结果（如Thompson映射的24到20压缩）缺乏规范统一的解释，作者旨在用极化代数的语言建立一个初等的、由碰撞本身直接生成的压缩原理，从而统一解释已知压缩现象并揭示新的反例。

Method: 作者引入"碰撞生成的压缩原理"：对齐次Keller映射构造碰撞所生成的极化子代数，该子代数携带非单射的Keller限制（restriction），并据此控制标准对称提升的维数。通过精确极化计算Thompson 24变量映射得到增长序列2,4,11,20,20，识别其子代数即MacFarlane的20维不变子空间。进一步构造350单项式的齐四次映射，在Q(i)上给出精确的梯度碰撞，并给出显式计算以检验Zhao消失猜想的失败。

Result: 规范恢复了Thompson映射的24到20压缩（子代数恰为MacFarlane的20维不变子空间）；给出了一个显式的350单项式齐四次映射使得Zhao的消失猜想失败，并给出Q(i)上的精确梯度碰撞；证明了在Q、Q(i)、C上不存在包含该碰撞的真不变线性子空间。

Conclusion: 碰撞生成的压缩原理可作为分析Keller映射维数压缩的规范工具；同时构造的反例表明Zhao消失猜想并不成立。作者声明不主张全局极小性，且所有显式计算均经精确计算验证。

Abstract: We formulate an elementary collision-generated compression principle for homogeneous Keller maps. In the language of polarization algebras, the construction takes the subalgebra generated by a collision; the additional point is that this subalgebra carries a noninjective Keller restriction and controls the dimension of the standard symmetric lift. For Thompson's 24-variable cubic-homogeneous map, exact polarization gives growth \(2,4,11,20,20\), and the resulting subalgebra is exactly MacFarlane's 20-dimensional invariant subspace. Thus the known \(24\)-to-\(20\) compression is recovered canonically from the collision itself. Although the resulting 40-variable lift had already been noted, we give an explicit 350-monomial homogeneous quartic for which Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture fails, together with an exact gradient collision over \(\Q(i)\). Over \(\Q\), \(\Q(i)\), and \(\C\), no proper invariant linear subspace containing this collision is available before the unchanged symmetric lift. No global minimality is claimed. The principal explicit calculations are checked by an accompanying exact calculation.

</details>


### [175] [Rigidity for Lie algebras of locally finite derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12567)
*Mohamed Ali Belabbas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了特征零域上有限生成交换代数A的导数代数Der_K(A)中，由有限个局部有限导子生成的任何Lie子代数都是有限维的。核心引理是：所有元素都为局部幂零的Lie子代数必是可解的，且当A化简时其导出长度至多为dim A且界是紧的。由此推导出仿射簇自同构群中由有限个连通代数子群生成子群的代数性判据，回答了Popov 2005年提出的问题，并给出了多项式控制系统有限维双线性实现的一个刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限生成交换代数上的导子Lie代数的结构，特别是解决局部有限导子所生成Lie子代数的有限维性问题，以及由此衍生出的代数群自同构子群的代数性问题（Popov猜想）和控制系统实现的代数刻画。

Method: 证明两个关键定理：(1) 所有元素局部幂零的Der_K(A)的Lie子代数必可解（化简时导出长度≤dim A且最优）；(2) 由有限个局部有限导子生成的Lie子代数必有限维。证明的核心技巧是引入一个共有限理想，使其与Spec A的所有结合点的闭包相交：在沿相应有限子概形二阶消失的导子子代数上，局部有限性强制导出局部幂零性；该子代数与L的交因此可解且余维有限，结合伴随作用的局部有限性得出有限维性。

Result: 证明了特征零域上有限生成交换代数A的Der_K(A)中由有限个局部有限导子生成的任何Lie子代数都是有限维的。给出了局部有限生成Lie子代数有限维、在A上局部有限作用、所有元素局部有限三者等价的充要条件。作为推论，对于仿射簇X，Aut(X)中由有限个连通代数子群生成的子群是代数的当且仅当其切代数生成的Lie代数的每个元素都局部有限；特别地解决了Popov关于两个幺幂单参数子群生成子群的问题。还刻画了具有基于状态坐标的多项式可观测量的精确有限维双线性实现的多项式控制系统。

Conclusion: 通过"局部有限性→局部幂零性"的转化机制和可解性引理，作者建立了一个统一的框架，将导子Lie代数的结构理论应用到代数群的自同构群和控制系统理论中。主要定理表明，有限生成交换代数上的"几乎可解"行为由局部幂零导子的可解性所支配，这一结构性结论具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finitely generated commutative algebra over a field $\mathbb K$ of characteristic zero. We prove that every finitely generated Lie subalgebra $L\subseteq\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally finite on $A$ is finite-dimensional. Consequently, for a Lie subalgebra generated by finitely many locally finite derivations, the following are equivalent: it is finite-dimensional, it acts locally finitely on $A$, and all its elements are locally finite. A key ingredient is a second theorem of independent interest: every Lie subalgebra of $\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally nilpotent is solvable; when $A$ is reduced, its derived length is at most $\dim A$, and this bound is sharp. For an affine variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field, we deduce that a subgroup of $\operatorname{Aut}(X)$ generated by finitely many connected algebraic subgroups is algebraic if and only if every element of the Lie algebra generated by their tangent algebras is locally finite. For two unipotent one-parameter subgroups, this provides an answer to a problem posed by Popov in 2005. Our results also characterize polynomial control systems admitting an exact finite-dimensional bilinear realization by polynomial observables containing the state coordinates. The proofs rest on the introduction of a cofinite ideal meeting the closure of every associated point of $\operatorname{Spec} A$: on the subalgebra of derivations vanishing to second order along the corresponding finite subscheme, local finiteness forces local nilpotence. The intersection of that subalgebra with $L$ is therefore solvable by the second theorem, and has finite codimension in $L$; together with local finiteness of the adjoint action, this yields finite-dimensionality.

</details>


### [176] [Tame fundamental groups of rigid spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12601)
*Piotr Achinger,Katharina Hübner,Marcin Lara,Jakob Stix*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入刚性空间X/K的驯顺平展基本群π_1^t(X/K)，证明当X为qcqs且K具有拓扑有限生成的驯顺Galois群（如代数闭域或局部域）时，该基本群是拓扑有限生成的；若X是严格半稳定形式概形的刚性一般纤维且其特殊纤维的光滑闭包具有射影snc紧化，则该基本群是拓扑有限表现的。证明依赖对数几何技术（扩展到有限生成幺半群之外）和adic空间映射的'垂直紧化'。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非阿基米德域上刚性空间的基本群有限性问题是算术几何中的核心课题。经典平展基本群的拓扑有限生成/有限表现性质已有广泛研究，但驯顺版本（tame版本）的基本群研究尚不充分。本文旨在建立刚性空间中驯顺平展基本群的有限性定理，填补该领域的空白。

Method: 1. 引入刚性空间上驯顺平展基本群π_1^t(X/K)的定义。2. 将对数几何技术扩展到非有限生成幺半群情形，证明驯顺对数平展基本群的类似有限性陈述。3. 利用adic空间映射的'垂直紧化'技术，将对基本群有限性的证明归结为对数几何情形。

Result: 1. 当X是qcqs且K具有拓扑有限生成的驯顺Galois群（如代数闭域或局部域）时，π_1^t(X/K)是拓扑有限生成的。2. 进一步地，当X是严格半稳定形式概形的刚性一般纤维且其特殊纤维光滑闭包具有射影snc紧化时，π_1^t(X/K)是拓扑有限表现的。

Conclusion: 本文建立了刚性空间驯顺平展基本群的拓扑有限生成与有限表现定理，拓展了对数几何方法的适用范围，并提出'垂直紧化'这一新工具，为刚性空间基本群理论提供了更完善的结果。

Abstract: We introduce the tame étale fundamental group $π_1^t(X/K)$ of a rigid space X over a non-archimedean field K. We show that if X is qcqs and K has topologically finitely generated tame Galois group (e.g. algebraically closed or a local field), then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely generated. If X is moreover the rigid generic fibre of a strictly semistable formal scheme such that the smooth locus of its special fibre admits a projective snc compactification, then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely presented. The proofs rely on techniques of logarithmic geometry (extended beyond its usual scope of finitely generated monoids), in particular on an analogous finiteness statement for the tame log étale fundamental group, and on the 'vertical compactification' of a map of adic spaces.

</details>


### [177] [Lower bounds for the Smith-Thom deficiency of Hilbert squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12644)
*Viatcheslav Kharlamov,Rareş Răsdeaconu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为极大非奇异实射影簇的 Hilbert 平方建立了 Smith–Thom 亏格的下界，推广了已知的曲面情形，并在每个维数至少为 2 的情况下提供了新的 Smith–Thom 极大性障碍。作为应用，证明了任意维数至少为 2 的实阿贝尔簇的 Hilbert 平方不是 Smith–Thom 极大的，并进一步证明了极大实阿贝尔簇的 Hilbert 平方的亏格随维数指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smith–Thom 极大性是实代数几何中关于实点和复点拓扑关系的重要概念。此前已知的结果仅覆盖曲面情形，高维情况下缺乏系统的 Smith–Thom 亏格下界估计，因此需要新的工具和障碍来理解高维实射影簇的 Hilbert 平方的拓扑结构。

Method: 通过建立 Hilbert 平方的 Smith–Thom 亏格的下界估计，利用代数几何与拓扑学工具，将问题归结为对实射影簇及其 Hilbert 平方同调结构的分析；同时针对实阿贝尔簇和笛卡尔积等特殊结构，运用具体拓扑不变量给出非极大性判定。

Result: (1) 建立了极大非奇异实射影簇 Hilbert 平方的 Smith–Thom 亏格下界，覆盖并推广了已知曲面情形；(2) 证明了维数至少为 2 的实阿贝尔簇的 Hilbert 平方均非 Smith–Thom 极大；(3) 极大实阿贝尔簇 Hilbert 平方的亏格随维数呈指数增长；(4) 对某些笛卡尔积的 Hilbert 平方也获得了非极大性结果。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一族新的 Smith–Thom 极大性障碍，表明高维实射影簇的 Hilbert 平方普遍不满足 Smith–Thom 极大性，且亏格下界具有良好的渐近行为，为实代数几何中 Smith–Thom 极大性理论的高维推广奠定了基础。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the Smith--Thom deficiency of the Hilbert square of a maximal nonsingular real projective variety. These bounds recover the known surface case and provide new obstructions to Smith-Thom maximality in every dimension at least two. As applications, we prove that the Hilbert square of any real abelian variety of dimension at least two is not Smith-Thom maximal. We further show that the deficiencies of the Hilbert squares of maximal real abelian varieties grow exponentially with the dimension. We also obtain nonmaximality results for the Hilbert squares of certain Cartesian products.

</details>


### [178] [Topological obstructions to geometric positivity and negativity on Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12705)
*Ping Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Calabi-Yau流形是否能在其底层拓扑上承托自然的几何正性或负性结构。作者在偶复维数n≥4（当n≥6时假设b2=1）的条件下，加强了Oguiso-Peternell定理，证明Calabi-Yau流形不同胚于弱Fano n-维复流形；该障碍同样适用于具有拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形。变换群的类比结果排除了具有带孤立不动点的Hamilton圆作用的辛流形。在负性方面，证明了Calabi-Yau流形的基本群不同构于Kähler双曲流形的基本群。这些结果共同展示了将Calabi-Yau流形与多种受几何正性/负性支配的基本类区分开的共同拓扑刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Calabi-Yau流形的拓扑结构能否支持自然的几何正性（如弱Fano、拟正全纯截面曲率）或几何负性（如Kähler双曲）结构，从而揭示Calabi-Yau流形与其他典范几何类之间的拓扑刚性边界。

Method: 通过加强Oguiso-Peternell定理，利用同胚不变量和拓扑障碍来排除Calabi-Yau流形与弱Fano n-维流形的同胚性；将同样的障碍方法推广到具有拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形及具有Hamilton圆作用（带孤立不动点）的辛流形；在负性方向上，利用基本群同构的不可能性排除与Kähler双曲流形的基本群同构。

Result: 证明偶复维数n≥4（b2=1当n≥6时）的Calabi-Yau流形不同胚于弱Fano n-维流形；该障碍适用于拟正全纯截面曲率流形；变换群类比排除带Hamilton圆作用及孤立不动点的辛流形；Calabi-Yau流形的基本群不同构于Kähler双曲流形的基本群。综合展现了Calabi-Yau流形与几何正性/负性流形类之间的共同拓扑刚性。

Conclusion: Calabi-Yau流形的拓扑不承载自然的几何正性或负性结构，它们在同胚意义下与弱Fano流形、拟正全纯截面曲率Kähler流形、特定辛流形及Kähler双曲流形均严格区分，揭示了这一重要几何类所特有的拓扑刚性。

Abstract: We study whether the topology underlying a Calabi-Yau manifold can support natural geometric positivity or negativity structures. In even complex dimension $n \geq 4$ (assuming $b_2=1$ when $n \geq 6$), we strengthen a theorem of Oguiso-Peternell by proving that a Calabi-Yau manifold is not homeomorphic to a weak Fano $n$-fold. The same obstruction applies to Kähler manifolds with quasi-positive holomorphic sectional curvature. A transformation-group analogue excludes, in particular, symplectic manifolds admitting Hamiltonian circle actions with isolated fixed points. On the negative side, we show that the fundamental group of a Calabi-Yau manifold is not isomorphic to that of a Kähler hyperbolic manifold. Taken together, these results exhibit a common topological rigidity separating Calabi-Yau manifolds from several fundamental classes governed by geometric positivity or negativity.

</details>


### [179] [Supersymmetry, differential operators of infinite order and theta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12846)
*Mikhail Kapranov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文用超对称性重新解释了M. Sato等人提出的通过无穷阶微分算子（DOI）证明Theta零值函数的模性的方法，指出任何奇超对称生成元的朴素指数都是DOI。具体而言，亏格为n的Riemann theta函数受超群OSp(1|2n)（及其metaplectic覆盖）在Siegel平面自然超加厚上的作用控制。n=2时对应三维N=1超共形群和自由无质量标量超多重态；n>2时得到拉格朗日Grassmannian上广义共形结构的超扩充。此外，在"on-shell"情形下奇超对称生成元满足类偶数的Heisenberg对易关系，这些运动方程升级为对应osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型自然分解的微分算子复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato在1972年提出的用DOI证明Theta零值函数模性的方法已被多位作者发展，但缺乏统一的物理解释。作者希望通过超对称框架为该抽象方法提供自然且统一的来源与诠释。

Method: 将超对称性引入为DOI的自然来源：任何奇超对称生成元的朴素指数即为DOI。利用超群OSp(1|2n)在Siegel平面超加厚上的作用来统一描述不同亏格n的情形，并分析on-shell情况下奇生成元满足的Heisenberg对易关系，进而建立相应的BGG型微分算子复形。

Result: 建立了Sato的DOI方法与超对称性之间的精确对应：n=2时该结构精确对应三维N=1超共形群控制的自由无质量标量超多重态（联合Laplace与Dirac方程）；n>2时得到拉格朗日Grassmannian上广义共形结构的超扩充。同时发现on-shell奇生成元满足类偶数的Heisenberg关系，运动方程升级为osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型复形。

Conclusion: 超对称性为Sato的DOI方法提供了自然的物理解释和统一框架，不仅深化了对Riemann theta函数模性的理解，还揭示了Theta函数理论、超共形几何与BGG分解之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In 1972, M. Sato proposed an approach to proving modularity of forms like Thetanullwerte by characterizing them via certain differential operators of infinite order (DOI) in the modular variable(s) alone. A DOI is an infinite series in derivatives decreasing so fast that it acts on holomorphic functions by a sheaf morphism. This approach was developed by several authors including Kashiwara, Kawai, Takei and Yoshida.
  We give an interpretation of this approach using supersymmetry which provides a natural source of DOIs: the naive exponential of any odd supersymmetry generator is a DOI. The case of the Riemann theta function of genus n is governed by the supergroup OSp(1|2n) (and its metaplectic cover) acting on a natural super-thickening of the Siegel plane. For n=2 this is the 3-dimensional N=1 superconformal group and the structure at hand is precisely the free massless scalar supermultiplet (combining the Laplace and Dirac equations). For n>2 we get a super-extension of the generalized conformal structure existing on the Lagrangian Grassmannian as on any Hermitian symmetric space.
  An additional interesting feature here is that the odd supersymmetry generators acting ``on-shell'' (i.e., in the space of solutions of the equations of motion) satisfy even-style Heisenberg commutation relations. These equations of motion upgrade to a complex of differential operators corresponding to a natural BGG-type resolution of the super-Weil representation of osp(1|2n).

</details>


### [180] [Algebraic cycles and Fano threefolds of genus 7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12950)
*Robert Laterveer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了亏格为7的一般素数Fano三维簇Y，在其自积Y×Y上构造了一个明确2-循环，该循环在Chow群A^4(Y×Y)中为Abel-Jacobi平凡但非挠元，从而证明Y不具有Shen-Vial意义下的乘法Chow-Künneth分解；同时证明任何Fano三维簇在模代数等价意义下都具有乘法Chow-Künneth分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Fano三维簇是否具有乘法Chow-Künneth分解（Mukai分解），这是Shen-Vial引入的关于Chow群环结构的精细问题。寻找不具有该分解的例子，以加深对Fano簇Chow理论的理解。

Method: 在亏格为7的素数Fano三维簇Y（具典范描述）上，通过其结构（如一维线性子簇族或相关几何）显式构造Y×Y上的2-循环，并利用Abel-Jacobi映射验证其平凡性但证明其在Chow群中非挠。此外，利用代数等价的商化论证，证明模代数等价的乘法Chow-Künneth分解存在性。

Result: 成功构造了一个在Y×Y上Abel-Jacobi平凡但非挠的2-循环，由此证明该Fano三维簇不具有乘法Chow-Künneth分解；另一方面，证明了所有Fano三维簇在代数等价模意义下都有乘法Chow-Künneth分解。

Conclusion: 乘法Chow-Künneth分解在Chow群级别（精确等价）并不普遍成立——存在（例如亏格7的素数Fano三维簇）不满足的例子；但在代数等价意义下该性质对所有Fano三维簇均成立，说明该分解的障碍源于Chow群中的高阶（超越）信息。

Abstract: Let $Y$ be a very general prime Fano threefold of genus 7. We exhibit an explicit 2-cycle on $Y\times Y$ that is Abel-Jacobi trivial but non-torsion in the Chow group $A^4(Y\times Y)$. As a consequence, $Y$ does not admit a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition, in the sense of Shen-Vial. We also show that any Fano threefold has a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition modulo algebraic equivalence.

</details>


### [181] [Tropical and algebraic elliptic plane curves with fixed j-invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13081)
*Alessio Cela,Sae Koyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过热带计数方法枚举具有固定$j$-不变量的平面良间距椭圆曲线，并结合Cela-Koyama的亏格1对应定理，给出了Pandharipande代数枚举的另一种证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Pandharipande的代数枚举提供一个替代性的证明途径，利用热带几何与对应定理建立联系。

Method: 采用热带几何方法对平面良间距椭圆曲线进行枚举，固定$j$-不变量（椭圆曲线的模不变量），然后结合Cela-Koyama的亏格1对应定理，将热带计数结果与代数枚举联系起来。

Result: 成功通过对固定$j$-不变量的热带有理曲线的热带枚举，结合对应定理，得到了Pandharipande代数枚举的替代证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一条从热带几何到代数枚举的新证明路径，为Pandharipande的经典结果提供了独立验证。

Abstract: We tropically enumerate planar well-spaced elliptic curves with a fixed $j$-invariant. Combined with the recent genus 1 correspondence theorem of Cela--Koyama, this yields an alternative proof of Pandharipande's algebraic enumeration.

</details>


### [182] [Equivalences via twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves from transverse Lagrangian fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13403)
*Moritz Hartlieb,Saket Shah*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用Kapustka-Kapustka的思想，通过Lagrangian纤维化在K3^[n]型和OG10型超凯勒流形的乘积上构造扭曲超全纯层，并以此证明了OG10型超凯勒流形的Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超凯勒流形上的标准猜想和导出等价性猜想是代数几何中的核心开放问题。作者希望利用Lagrangian纤维化的几何结构来构造超全纯层，从而为证明这些著名猜想提供新的工具。

Method: 采用Kapustka-Kapustka的方法，通过Lagrangian纤维化在K3^[n]型与OG10型超凯勒流形的乘积空间上构造扭曲（twisted）超全纯层（twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves），利用这些层的特殊性质来建立所需的范畴等价和上同调结构。

Result: 成功构造了所需的扭曲超全纯层，并以此为工具证明了OG10型超凯勒流形满足Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想。

Conclusion: 验证了Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想在OG10型超凯勒流形上成立，扩展了超凯勒几何中标准猜想的已知范围，证明了Lagrangian纤维化方法在构造超全纯层上的有效性。

Abstract: Following ideas of Kapustka-Kapustka, we use Lagrangian fibrations to construct twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves on products of hyperkähler manifolds of K3$^{[n]}$- and OG10-type. As applications, we prove the Lefschetz standard conjecture and the D-equivalence conjecture for hyperkähler manifolds of OG10-type.

</details>


### [183] [Sites and Grothendieck Topologies, Sites and Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13405)
*Arvid Siqveland*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了理解Grothendieck topos定义及其应用所需的最少范畴论知识，阐述了site和sheaf的基本性质，并将其应用于模空间理论。文中证明了对于具有特定性质的范畴C，可以构造一个对象的scheme范畴，该范畴是一个真范畴（proper category）而非像stack那样的2-范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grothendieck topos是代数几何中模空间理论的重要工具，但其定义涉及大量范畴论预备知识。作者旨在提供一个最小化的范畴论基础来理解topos，并解决模空间中stack作为2-范畴带来的复杂性，从而构造一个真范畴而非2-范畴的对象scheme范畴。

Method: 本文采用概念性/理论性方法：首先系统性地介绍定义Grothendieck topos所必需的最少范畴论概念；然后阐述site（覆盖系）和sheaf（层）的基本性质；最后证明在满足明确陈述性质的范畴C上，可以构造一个对象的scheme范畴，该范畴是proper category而非2-范畴。

Result: 作者成功证明：对于具有明确陈述性质的范畴C，可以构造一个scheme范畴（对象为C中对象的scheme），并且该范畴是一个真范畴而非2-范畴，从而避免了stack理论中2-范畴带来的层级复杂性。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解Grothendieck topos提供了精简的范畴论框架，并将模空间理论从stack的2-范畴框架简化为真范畴框架，这可能为模空间的构造和研究提供更简洁的途径。

Abstract: We give the minimum of category theory necessary for understanding the definition and applications of Grothendieck topos. We state the basic properties of sites and sheaves, and give applications to the theory of moduli. We prove that for categories $\mathbf C$ with explicit stated properties, we can construct a category of schemes of objects in $\mathbf C$ which is a proper category rather than a $2$-category as in the case of stacks.

</details>

### [184] [Identifying changing partial differential equations using Sampled Local WeakIdent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12479)
*Wenbo Hao,Mengyi Tang,Sung Ha Kang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出SLW-Ident框架，用于从单一给定的数据集中识别随时间或空间变化的控制方程（PDE）。该框架通过从整个域中采样局部小块，对每个块进行方程识别，并利用残差误差来寻找不同方程之间的转换区域，从而恢复分段/渐变PDE及其系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法使用有限元近似来表示变系数方程，但这类方法在计算上可能开销较大或对局部扰动敏感。作者希望探索一种更轻量、局部化的微分方程识别方法，既能有效识别局部方程，又能整合全局信息以处理跨区域的方程转变。

Method: SLW-Ident由三部分组成：(1) 引入Local WeakIdent方法，利用局部弱形式识别方程，计算高效但对局部扰动敏感；(2) 在整个域中采样若干小块（patch），分别识别每个块的方程，并通过残差误差判断方程之间的转换边界，据此划分"单方程区域"；(3) 在每个单方程区域内，选取出现频率最高的方程作为该区域的识别结果，并可进一步求解常数系数及变系数PDE。作者还提供了不确定性量化理论，建立了主导支撑选择的统计误差与样本块数量之间的关系。

Result: 通过多项数值实验表明，SLW-Ident能够在含噪声数据下准确恢复"单方程区域"的划分，并正确识别具有变系数、含变化规律的PDE控制方程。

Conclusion: SLW-Ident提供了一种局部采样结合全局信息整合的稳健框架，可有效处理方程在不同区域发生转变的场景，兼具局部识别的高效性和全局识别的鲁棒性，并具备理论支撑。

Abstract: We propose Sampled Local WeakIdent (SLW-Ident), a framework for identifying changing governing equations from a single set of given data. Different from a typical approach of using finite element based approximation to represent varying coefficients, this paper explores a local approach in identification of differential equations. First, we present the power of Local WeakIdent which gives good local identification and is also computationally efficient with a small patch size, yet it can be sensitive to local perturbations. We propose SLW-Ident which stabilizes the identification process and also incorporates global information: we first sample patches in the whole given domain, identify equations for each sampled patch, then use residual error of these equations to find the transitions between different equations. We refer to a region where the support of the identified equation does not change to be a region of one equation. Within each region of one equation, we pick the most frequently identified equation as the identified equation, and find constant as well as varying coefficient PDEs within each region of one equation. This is justified by an uncertainty quantification theory that gives the relation between statistical error of dominant support selection and the number of patches. We provide various numerical experiments showing that SLW-Ident accurately recovers the regions of one equation and the governing equations for changing PDEs with varying coefficients even with noisy given data.

</details>


### [185] [BTF-PINN: Enforcing Dirichlet Boundary Conditions Without Boundary Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12823)
*Wenyu Dong,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了BTF-PINN（免边界训练物理信息神经网络），一种仅需内部域采样即可求解齐次Dirichlet边值问题的方法，无需边界训练、边界惩罚或边界适配参数化，通过在损失函数中嵌入本质边界条件实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齐次Dirichlet边值问题是非插值方法（如PINN）求解Dirichlet问题时的核心难点，传统方法需要边界训练或边界惩罚，增加了实现复杂度和计算成本。

Method: 设计了一种仅含内部域的变分公式，将本质边界条件嵌入到新型免边界损失函数中；通过内蕴强制性分析证明了该变分形式与原边值问题的等价性，给出了残差权重的锐利阈值条件，并建立了基于泛函强制性的收敛分析。

Result: 在高维问题、不规则几何和各向异性椭圆方程上的数值实验验证了BTF-PINN的有效性；与标准边界惩罚PINN的对比表明，BTF-PINN在边界迹精度上具有更优表现。

Conclusion: BTF-PINN为齐次Dirichlet边值问题提供了一种简洁、高效的内部纯策略求解方案，理论上有等价性证明和收敛性保证，实践上在多种挑战性场景中优于传统边界惩罚方法。

Abstract: The homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problem captures the core difficulty of solving Dirichlet problems with non-interpolatory methods. We propose BTF-PINN (Boundary-Training-Free Physics-Informed Neural Network), an interior-only strategy for solving homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problems that requires no boundary training, boundary penalties, or boundary-conforming parametrizations. The key idea is to embed the essential boundary condition into a newly designed boundary-free loss function. We prove the equivalence between the proposed interior-only variational formulation and the original boundary value problem, establish a sharp threshold condition for the residual weight, and develop a convergence analysis based on the coercivity of the functional. Numerical experiments on high-dimensional problems, irregular geometries, and anisotropic elliptic equations demonstrate the effectiveness of BTF-PINN. Comparisons with standard boundary-penalty PINNs further show that BTF-PINN achieves superior boundary trace accuracy.

</details>


### [186] [Finite element approximation of the linearized Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12833)
*Ali Mesforush,Stig Larsson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性化Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程的数值逼近方法，采用标准有限元方法进行空间离散，并在Wiener过程的协方差算子满足适当假设的前提下证明了强收敛估计。同时研究了向后欧拉时间步进方法，分析框架基于解析半群理论，主要工作集中在确定性Cahn-Hilliard方程的详细误差界推导。结果可视为对非线性Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程mild解中随机卷积部分的逼近研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程是描述相分离动力学的随机偏微分方程，其数值逼近（包括空间离散和时间离散）的强收敛性分析具有重要的理论和实际意义。由于非线性方程的复杂性，作者选择先分析线性化方程，并重点研究随机卷积的近似性质。

Method: 采用标准有限元方法对空间变量进行离散，利用解析半群框架进行分析。对Wiener过程的协方差算子施加适当假设以证明强收敛估计，并使用向后欧拉方法进行时间离散。主要推导工作集中于对应的确定性Cahn-Hilliard方程的详细误差界。

Result: 在Wiener过程协方差算子的适当假设下，证明了有限元空间离散和向后欧拉时间离散的强收敛估计，并获得了确定性Cahn-Hilliard方程的详细误差界。

Conclusion: 结果表明该数值格式在解析半群框架下具有良好的收敛性质，为非线性Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程中随机卷积部分的逼近提供了理论保证。

Abstract: The linearized Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation is
  discretized in the spatial variables by a standard finite element
  method. Strong convergence estimates are proved under suitable
  assumptions on the covariance operator of the Wiener process,
  which is driving the equation. The backward Euler time stepping is
  also studied. The analysis is set in a framework based on analytic
  semigroups. The main effort is spent on proving detailed error
  bounds for the corresponding deterministic Cahn-Hilliard
  equation. The results should be interpreted as results on
  approximation of the stochastic convolution, which is a part of
  the mild solution of the nonlinear Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation.

</details>


### [187] [A robust and optimal method for a sixth-order elliptic singular perturbation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12886)
*Mingqing Chen,Jianguo Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对含小参数0<ι<1的梯度弹性Kirchhoff板（GEKP）模型的鲁棒非协调有限元方法。该方法在离散空间中使用H^3三次有限元，而在双线性形式的低阶项和右端项中使用插值映射到H^2非协调有限元空间。该数值方法对参数ι具有鲁棒性，无需任何稳定项即可对h实现一致收敛。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含有小参数的梯度弹性Kirchhoff板模型，现有数值方法可能对该参数缺乏鲁棒性，导致数值求解不稳定或需要额外的人工稳定化技术。作者旨在开发一种无需稳定项即可对参数ι鲁棒且对网格尺寸h一致收敛的数值方法。

Method: 采用H^3三次有限元作为离散空间，同时在双线性形式的低阶项和右端项中引入到H^2非协调有限元空间的插值映射。通过在低阶项中使用非协调插值，避免了稳定项的需求，从而实现对参数ι的鲁棒性和对h的一致一阶收敛。最后通过数值实验验证理论分析。

Result: 所提出的有限元方法对参数ι是鲁棒的，在无需任何稳定化项的情况下，对网格尺寸h实现了一致的一阶收敛（O(h)）。数值实验与理论结果一致，验证了该方法在梯度弹性Kirchhoff板模型中的有效性与稳健性。

Conclusion: 该文提出的鲁棒非协调有限元方法有效解决了梯度弹性Kirchhoff板模型数值求解中对小参数ι的鲁棒性问题，避免了稳定化项的引入，实现了均匀一阶收敛，为该类奇异摄动板问题提供了可靠的数值求解方案。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a robust nonconforming finite method for a gradient-elastic Kirchhoff plate (GEKP) model with a small parameter $0<ι<1$. While the method employs an $H^3$ cubic finite element for the discrete space, it employs an interpolation mapping into an $H^2$ nonconforming finite element space in both the lower-order terms of the bilinear form and the right-hand side. Notably, the numerical method is robust with respect to the parameter $ι$ and uniformly convergent to order $h$ without any stabilized terms. Finally, some numerical experiments are performed to verify the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [188] [A Counterexample to Robust Second-Order Convergence of the Strang Projector-Splitting Integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12893)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明经典的Strang投影分裂积分器在低秩近似中，当最小保留奇异值趋于零时，误差分析只能保证一阶收敛，且这一阶与二阶之间的差距是内在的、无法通过标准假设消除的。作者构造了具体的3×3矩阵微分方程反例，说明即使满足均匀有界性、Lipschitz、切向缺陷和正则性等所有标准假设，精确子流的Strang方法仍会产生非零的h²局部误差项，从而排除了稳健的二阶收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Strang投影分裂积分器在实际中广泛观察到二阶收敛，但现有的误差分析在最小保留奇异值趋于零时只能证明一阶收敛。理解这一阶-二阶差距是否本质性（而非分析技术不足所致）对于低秩数值方法的理论发展和实际应用至关重要。

Method: 作者通过构造特定的3×3矩阵微分方程反例进行研究。该反例满足所有标准理论假设（C²时间正则性、矩阵变量光滑性、秩二初始数据、均匀有界性、Lipschitz条件、切向缺陷和有界性约束），然后分析精确子流Strang方法在一个由四个Strang步骤组成的周期强迫循环上的局部误差，展示其非零的h²项，并通过循环重复论证排除与保留奇异值无关的全局二阶误差界。

Result: 构造的反例表明：(1) 精确子流Strang方法的局部误差在一个四步周期强迫循环中具有非零的h²项；(2) 该周期循环的重复排除了常数和步长阈值均与保留奇异值无关的全局二阶收敛界；(3) 机理是小奇异值相关的因子方向的快速旋转——一阶一致性得以保持，但变化中的投影空间阻止了对对称Strang复合本应产生的相消效应。

Conclusion: 在标准假设条件下，经典投影分裂Strang方法的稳健一阶结果无法被升级为稳健二阶。这一阶-二阶之间的差距是算法本身的固有属性，而非误差分析技术不足所致。任何希望获得稳健二阶收敛的改进都必须引入额外的假设或修改算法本身。

Abstract: The classical Strang projector-splitting integrator is widely observed to converge with order two, whereas the error analysis that remains uniform as the smallest singular value retained in the low-rank approximation tends to zero proves only order one. We show that this gap is intrinsic under the standard assumptions. We construct $3\times3$ matrix differential equations that are $C^2$ in time and smooth in the matrix variable, with rank-two initial data that satisfy uniform boundedness, Lipschitz, tangency-defect, and regularity bounds. Nevertheless, the exact-subflow Strang method has a nonzero $h^2$ term in the local error over one periodic forcing cycle consisting of four Strang steps. Repetition of that cycle rules out a global second-order bound whose constant and stepsize threshold are independent of the retained singular values. The mechanism is a rapid rotation of the factor directions associated with the small singular value: first-order consistency is preserved, but the changing projection spaces prevent the cancellation normally expected from a symmetric Strang composition. Hence the robust first-order result cannot, under these assumptions alone, be upgraded to robust second order for the classical projector-splitting method.

</details>


### [189] [Analysis of Error Propagation in Autoencoder-Based Reduced-Order Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13132)
*Jingyi Zhang,Gwanghyun Jo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过路径积分恒等式分析神经ODE降阶模型的长期外推误差，分离局部偏差注入与放大效应，并用多步Jacobian范数量化传输敏感性，在Burgers和Gray-Scott系统中识别出两种不同的误差演化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经ODE降阶模型在局部预测上具有相当精度，但长期外推行为差异显著。作者希望从理论上解释这种差异的来源，区分局部误差注入与学习到的潜动态中的误差放大机制。

Method: 开发路径积分恒等式将局部偏差注入与放大分离；利用多步Jacobian范数量化传输敏感性并区分不同传播机制；在Burgers和Gray-Scott系统上进行实验验证。

Result: Burgers系统中预测误差保持有界，主要与持续存在的局部偏差相关；Gray-Scott系统在外推过程中表现出显著的误差放大，此时Jacobian范数更多作为敏感性诊断指标而非物理预测精度的直接指示器。

Conclusion: 神经ODE降阶模型的长期外推行为由局部误差注入与潜动态放大机制共同决定，不同物理系统呈现不同主导机制；Jacobian范数在误差放大机制下的解释需谨慎，其作为敏感性诊断工具的价值与直接误差预测之间的界限被明确区分。

Abstract: Neural ODE reduced-order models often achieve comparable local prediction accuracy, yet their long-horizon extrapolation behavior can differ substantially. To analyze this discrepancy, we develop a path-integral identity that separates local discrepancy injection from amplification in the learned latent dynamics. The associated multi-step Jacobian norms quantify transport sensitivity and distinguish different propagation regimes. Experiments on the Burgers and Gray--Scott systems exhibit two distinct patterns of error evolution. In Burgers systems, prediction errors remain bounded and are primarily associated with persistent local discrepancies. In contrast, Gray--Scott systems exhibit pronounced amplification during extrapolation, where Jacobian norms serve as sensitivity diagnostics rather than direct indicators of physical prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [190] [Performance Evaluation of an Adaptive Quadrature and a Double Exponential Formula Using Arbitrary-Precision Floating-Point Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13187)
*Tomonori Kouya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文使用GNU MPFR库实现了AQE11D（Ninomiya自适应9点Newton-Cotes规则扩展）和Takahasi-Mori双指数（DE）公式，并在Kahaner的21个测试问题上进行了评估。在10^-50和10^-100两种绝对容差下，AQE11D在全部21个问题上达到目标精度，但在强端点奇异性（如1/√x）下需要约5.4×10^7次函数求值，比DE公式多约7×10^4倍。DE公式在两种容差下收敛于18个问题，展示了其处理端点奇异性的优势，但在积分区间内部存在奇异性时会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对高精度数值积分需求的增长（如高精度科学计算），需要比较不同自适应数值积分算法在极高精度要求（10^-50和10^-100）下的性能和鲁棒性，以指导算法选择。

Method: 使用GNU MPFR（任意精度浮点可靠库）实现两种数值积分算法：AQE11D（Ninomiya自适应9点Newton-Cotes规则，扩展了高阶规则序列）和Takahasi-Mori双指数（DE）公式。在Kahaner标准的21个测试问题上，分别在10^-50和10^-100绝对容差下进行评估比较。

Result: AQE11D在两种容差下均能在全部21个测试问题上达到目标精度；但对于强端点奇异性（如1/√x），在10^-100容差下需约5.4×10^7次函数求值，比DE公式多约7×10^4倍。DE公式在两种容差下可在18个问题上收敛，但对积分区间内部存在奇异性的问题失效。

Conclusion: AQE11D具有普遍适用性（能处理所有21个测试问题），但对端点奇异性效率较低；DE公式对端点奇异性非常高效，但对内部奇异性缺乏处理能力。两种算法互补，实际应用时需根据被积函数的奇异性特征选择合适的算法。

Abstract: Using arbitrary-precision arithmetic provided by the GNU Multiple Precision Floating-Point Reliable Library, we implement AQE11D---that is, Ninomiya's adaptive 9-point Newton--Cotes rule extended with a sequence of higher-order rules---and Takahasi and Moris' double exponential (DE) formula. We evaluate them for Kahaner's 21 test problems. For both absolute tolerances $10^{-50}$ and $10^{-100}$, AQE11D attains target accuracy on all 21 problems; however, for strong endpoint singularity such as $1/\sqrt{x}$, it requires about $5.4\times10^{7}$ function evaluations at $10^{-100}$, roughly $7\times10^{4}$ times as many as the DE formula. The formula converges on 18 problems at both tolerances, demonstrating its strength against endpoint singularities but also its failure, as it stands, on problems with a singularity inside the integration interval.

### [191] [Stochastic resistive Hall--MHD with current fluctuations: martingale weak solutions via a convergent structure-preserving finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13407)
*Agus L. Soenjaya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界凸多面体域上随机电阻Hall-MHD系统，设计了基于兼容离散de Rham复形的线性隐式、结构保持混合有限元方法，该方法的磁Gauss定律精确保持，并证明了离散解子序列收敛于有限能量的弱鞅解，从而为带curl型噪声的随机Hall-MHD系统提供了构造性存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Hall-MHD系统在等离子体物理中有重要应用，但此前缺乏该系统的存在性理论和非线性完美导电边界条件下的数值方法设计。需要一种既能处理随机扰动（包括动量方程中的乘性Gaussian噪声和感应方程中来自广义Ohm定律的curl型结构噪声），又能保证磁场散度约束的结构保持数值方法。

Method: 基于兼容的离散de Rham复形，开发了完全离散的线性隐式结构保持混合有限元方法。该方法：(1) 处理动量方程中的乘性Gaussian迫使与感应方程中的curl型结构噪声；(2) 通过混合有限元框架精确保持磁Gauss定律；(3) 在非线性完美导电边界条件下进行离散化。

Result: 证明了离散解的子序列收敛到有限能量的弱鞅解，从而获得随机Hall-MHD系统（含curl型噪声）的构造性存在性结果。数值实验展示了随机动力学行为、精确的散度保持特性及方法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将结构保持有限元方法推广到带curl型随机噪声的Hall-MHD系统，同时建立了数值方法收敛性与系统弱解存在性之间的联系，为随机MHD类问题的数值分析和理论分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study the stochastic resistive Hall--magnetohydrodynamic (Hall--MHD) system on bounded convex polyhedral domains, subject to nonlinear perfectly conducting boundary conditions. The system is driven by multiplicative Gaussian forcing in the momentum equation together with structured curl-type noise in the induction equation arising from stochastic perturbations of the resistive and Hall parts in the generalised Ohm law. We develop a fully discrete, linearly implicit, structure-preserving mixed finite element method based on a compatible discrete de Rham complex, which preserves the magnetic Gauss law exactly. We prove subsequential convergence of the discrete solutions to a finite-energy weak martingale solution, thereby obtaining a constructive existence result for the stochastic Hall--MHD system with curl-type noise. Numerical experiments illustrate the stochastic dynamics, exact divergence preservation, and convergence of the method.

</details>
